If port 4174 is already held by another server, the preview exits immediately, but `fetch(base)` can still succeed against the foreign server. The script then screenshots whatever that server returns, or fails with a confusing `data-presentation` timeout. `scripts/verify.mjs` guards against this with `preview.exitCode !== null`, and this script does not. The `close` promise never resolves, so the `FAIL:` message and the `finally` cleanup are bypassed.
